Welcome back to the Draft Viewer! Today we've got a triple *Masters Edition IV* draft from the **Magic Online** Championship Series Season 1 Championship! You can read more about the MOCS [here](/en/articles/uncategorised/undated/magic-online-championship-series), and ME4 Release Events are [going on right now](/en/articles/feature/2011/01-04-magic-online-masters-edition-iv-release-events).

[![](https://media.magic.wizards.com/image_legacy_migration/images/magic/daily/activity/da628_ss.jpg)](https://web.archive.org/web/20110119000000/http://gatherer.wizards.com/magic/draftools/draftviewer.asp?draftid=mtgo1977095)

## top 8 bracket

**ponza**

MrSkyBlue

**FANFARRAO**

### Finals

**ponza, 2-1**

### Champion

**ponza, 2-1**
